Generated by `node scripts/check-unused-type-members.audit.mjs --inventory`.

411 need a recorded disposition. Closure is
`--check`, which fails every one of them until its evidence is recorded.

| extensions/pi-claude-marketplace/bridges/hooks/stage.ts:227:3 | WriteHookConfigResult | written | unread | - | Left standing by the bridges/hooks repair after inspection; the recorded evidence it inherited was wrong about the tree. The whole result IS deep-compared: `assert.deepStrictEqual(write, { written: true, path: expectedPath })` appears seven times in `tests/bridges/hooks/stage.test.ts` and twice in `tests/bridges/hooks/index.test.ts`, over a value production code produced. The analysis credits neither member through those nine sites, while the sibling `RemoveHookConfigResult.removed` -- deep-compared the same way in the same file at :786 and :804 -- carries two `deep-comparison` witnesses. The difference is the callee: `removeHookConfig` is a directly declared exported function, whereas `writeHookConfig` is the closure `createWriteHookConfig` returns, so `assertionSummary`'s `lineage: "production"` demand is settled by `isProductionDerived`, whose bounded backward search does not reach production through a factory-returned closure. Deleting the slot would delete an observed member and weaken nine assertions, so this row is an analyzer under-credit for a bounded analyzer plan to model -- carry lineage through a factory-returned closure -- and not a source repair. |
